--- Commit Message ---
LU-19125 lfs: add multithreading to lfs project command

Add parallel directory traversal support to 'lfs project' and move the
core project xattr walk into reusable llapi helpers.

Public library API (lustreapi.h):
  llapi_project_xattr_get() / llapi_project_xattr_fget()
  ll_atomic_compare_exchange()

Internal helpers (lustreapi_internal.h / liblustreapi_project.c):
  struct llapi_project_handle_control
  enum llapi_project_flags (SET_INHERIT, SET_PROJID, KEEP_PROJID,
    RECURSIVE, DIRONLY, LIST)
  llapi_project_iterate()
  llapi_project_xattr_set_stat() / llapi_project_xattr_clear_stat()

Tool-private flags (lfs_project.h):
  LFS_PROJECT_ASSIGN_PROJID, LFS_PROJECT_NEWLINE

lfs project changes:
- Replace the custom linked-list walk with llapi_find_with_cb() /
  llapi_project_iterate().
- Auto-calculate thread count from MDT count and CPU count; clamp the
  default to 255 so it fits phc_thread_count (__u8).
- Add -j/--threads (1..255).
- Fix the long_opts[] terminator in lfs_project() so getopt_long()
  stops correctly.

Threading behavior:
  lfs project /dir          -> 1 thread (non-recursive)
  lfs project -r /dir       -> auto-calculated (capped at 255)
  lfs project -r -d /dir    -> 1 thread (-d overrides -r)
  lfs project -j 8 -r /dir  -> 8 threads (user override)

Fixes: c939d2f23048 ("LU-18660 utils: add long opts for 'lfs project'")
Test-Parameters: testlist=sanity env=ONLY=190d serverversion=2.15

Signed-off-by: Chakshu Kansal <ckansal@ddn.com>

Patch Set 14:

(3 comments)

AI code review results below, please doublecheck

Mostly good, a couple of style comments inline. One whole-patch point: of the six new public entry points, llapi_project_xattr_set(), _fset(), _clear() and _fclear() have no caller anywhere in the tree - lfs project goes through the internal llapi_project_xattr_set_stat()/_clear_stat() instead - and sanity/190d only exercises the lfs command line. So the special-file branch of llapi_project_xattr_set()/_clear(), which was the subject of a fix during review, ships with nothing to catch a regression. Could a small case be added that calls the public wrappers directly on a file, a directory and a symlink?

Some concerns, see inline comments. The main one is that `lfs project -r` now uses the parallel find path by default, and that path can overrun a heap buffer on deep trees. The root cause is in liblustreapi_pfind.c and predates this patch, so a small prerequisite fix there would be enough. The other comments are minor and can go in the same refresh.
